    Quest Drop Rates (ER)

    Data Version: 2.0.2

    Notes/Observations

    • This is for Endless Ragnarok quests, for base game quests, head to this page
    • If you are using this anywhere, give proper credits to Nenkai

    If you need to find a specific quest, Ctrl+F and search by quest name.

    >> SPOILER WARNING (Full Quest List) - CLICK TO EXPAND

    Chaos¶

    A Trial of Two Eternities (408301)¶

    First Clear Rewards:
- Fatebreaker V+

[Sub Missions Rewards]
Clear within 10 minute(s): - x10 Knickknack Voucher
Don't get inflicted with ailments too often: - x1 Azurite Shard
Don't get hit by Wrath of Star and Crown: - x3 Legendary Merit

    Jumbo Crab's Title Fight (408302)¶

    First Clear Rewards:
- Sumo Force

[Sub Missions Rewards]
Perform at least 10 link attacks: - x1 Legendary Merit
Don't fall off the stage too often: - x2 Glittercrystal Cluster
Clear within 6 minute(s): - x3 Glittercrystal Cluster

[Clear Rewards]
* Lot #1 (Total Weight: 10000)
